Class 1 · Participants in an MTurk survey were randomly assigned among three labels for the political option; the consumer-products option did not vary.

O1 · Study 6 observational component — Comfort with conversation topics

Class 5 · The across-topic Pew analyses compare the same respondents’ comfort and relate comfort to measured conflict avoidance; apart from the separately inventoried politics/Trump split, these are observational associations. Randomized item order is a procedural order control with no order-effect estimand or reported analysis, so it is not treated as a distinct experiment.

Experiment design: N/A

E7 · Study 7 — Conversations on Issues

Class 1 · Participants were randomly assigned to one of four conversation topics and asked one comfort outcome.

Experiment design: completely randomized experiment; survey experiment
